a|icon:lock[title=Fixed at build time] [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-serializer]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-serializer[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.serializer+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.serializer+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
The fully-qualified class name of the serializer implementation used for message serialization and deserialization. The class must implement `io.quarkiverse.reactive.messaging.nats.jetstream.client.message.Serializer`. The default is `io.quarkiverse.reactive.messaging.nats.jetstream.client.message.JacksonSerializer`.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_SERIALIZER+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_SERIALIZER+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|string
|`+++io.quarkiverse.reactive.messaging.nats.jetstream.client.message.JacksonSerializer+++`

a|icon:lock[title=Fixed at build time] [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-devservices-shared]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-devservices-shared[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.devservices.shared+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.devservices.shared+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Indicates if the NATS JetStream broker managed by Quarkus Dev Services is shared. When shared, Quarkus looks for running containers using label-based service discovery. If a matching container is found, it is used, and so a second one is not started. Otherwise, Dev Services for NATS JetStream starts a new container.

The discovery uses the `quarkus-dev-service-jetstream` label. The value is configured using the `service-name` property.

Container sharing is only used in dev mode.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DEVSERVICES_SHARED+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DEVSERVICES_SHARED+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|boolean
|`+++true+++`

a|icon:lock[title=Fixed at build time] [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-devservices-service-name]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-devservices-service-name[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.devservices.service-name+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.devservices.service-name+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
The value of the `quarkus-dev-service-jetstream` label attached to the started container. This property is used when `shared` is set to `true`. In this case, before starting a container, Dev Services for NATS JetStream looks for a container with the `quarkus-dev-service-jetstream` label set to the configured value. If found, it will use this container instead of starting a new one. Otherwise, it starts a new container with the `quarkus-dev-service-jetstream` label set to the specified value.

This property is used when you need multiple shared NATS JetStream brokers.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DEVSERVICES_SERVICE_NAME+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DEVSERVICES_SERVICE_NAME+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|string
|`+++nats+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-thread-pool-virtual-threads]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-thread-pool-virtual-threads[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.thread-pool.virtual-threads+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.thread-pool.virtual-threads+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Whether every task runs on a new virtual thread instead of a pooled platform thread. When enabled, the core size, max size, queue size and keep alive are ignored. Requires Java 21 or later; on older Java versions a warning is logged and the pooled platform threads are used.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_THREAD_POOL_VIRTUAL_THREADS+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_THREAD_POOL_VIRTUAL_THREADS+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|boolean
|`+++false+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-max-acknowledge-pending]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-max-acknowledge-pending[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".max-acknowledge-pending+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".max-acknowledge-pending+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Defines the maximum number of messages; without acknowledgment; that can be outstanding. Once this limit is reached; message delivery will be suspended. This limit applies across all of the consumer's bound subscriptions. A value of -1 means there can be any number of pending acknowledgments (i.e.; no flow control). The default is 1000.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_ACKNOWLEDGE_PENDING+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_ACKNOWLEDGE_PENDING+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|long
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-max-deliver]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-max-deliver[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".max-deliver+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".max-deliver+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
The maximum number of times a specific message delivery will be attempted. Applies to any message that is re-sent due to acknowledgment policy (i.e.; due to a negative acknowledgment or no acknowledgment sent by the client). The default is -1 (redeliver until acknowledged). Messages that have reached the maximum delivery count will stay in the stream.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_DELIVER+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_DELIVER+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|long
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-backoff]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-backoff[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".backoff+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".backoff+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
A sequence of delays controlling the re-delivery of messages on acknowledgment timeout (but not on nak). The sequence length must be less than or equal to MaxDeliver. If backoff is not set; a timeout will result in immediate re-delivery. E.g.; MaxDeliver=5 backoff=++[++5s; 30s; 300s; 3600s; 84000s++]++ will re-deliver a message 5 times over one day. When MaxDeliver is larger than the backoff list; the last delay in the list will apply for the remaining deliveries. Note that backoff is NOT applied to naked messages. A nak will result in immediate re-delivery unless nakWithDelay is used to set the re-delivery delay explicitly. When BackOff is set; it overrides AckWait entirely. The first value in the BackOff determines the AckWait value.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__BACKOFF+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__BACKOFF+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|list of link:https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/time/Duration.html[Duration]
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-replay-policy]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-replay-policy[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".replay-policy+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".replay-policy+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
If the policy is ReplayOriginal; the messages in the stream will be pushed to the client at the same rate they were originally received; simulating the original timing. If the policy is ReplayInstant (default); the messages will be pushed to the client as fast as possible while adhering to the acknowledgment policy; Max Ack Pending; and the client's ability to consume those messages.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__REPLAY_POLICY+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__REPLAY_POLICY+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
a|`instant`, `original`
|`+++instant+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-push-options-flow-control]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-push-options-flow-control[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".push-options.flow-control+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".push-options.flow-control+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Enables per-subscription flow control using a sliding-window protocol. This protocol relies on the server and client exchanging messages to regulate when and how many messages are pushed to the client. This one-to-one flow control mechanism works in tandem with the one-to-many flow control imposed by MaxAckPending across all subscriptions bound to a consumer


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_FLOW_CONTROL+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_FLOW_CONTROL+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|boolean
|`+++false+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-push-options-idle-heartbeat]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-consumers-consumers-push-options-idle-heartbeat[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".push-options.idle-heartbeat+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.consumers."consumers".push-options.idle-heartbeat+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
If the idle heartbeat period is set, the server will regularly send a status message to the client (i.e. when the period has elapsed) while there are no new messages to send. This lets the client know that the JetStream service is still up and running, even when there is no activity on the stream. The message status header will have a code of 100. Unlike FlowControl, it will have no reply to address. It may have a description such ++\++"Idle Heartbeat++\++". Note that this heartbeat mechanism is all handled transparently by supported clients and does not need to be handled by the application


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_IDLE_HEARTBEAT+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_IDLE_HEARTBEAT+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|link:https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/time/Duration.html[Duration] link:#duration-note-anchor-quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging[icon:question-circle[title=More information about the Duration format]]
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-max-acknowledge-pending]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-max-acknowledge-pending[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".max-acknowledge-pending+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".max-acknowledge-pending+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Defines the maximum number of messages; without acknowledgment; that can be outstanding. Once this limit is reached; message delivery will be suspended. This limit applies across all of the consumer's bound subscriptions. A value of -1 means there can be any number of pending acknowledgments (i.e.; no flow control). The default is 1000.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_ACKNOWLEDGE_PENDING+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_ACKNOWLEDGE_PENDING+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|long
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-max-deliver]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-max-deliver[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".max-deliver+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".max-deliver+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
The maximum number of times a specific message delivery will be attempted. Applies to any message that is re-sent due to acknowledgment policy (i.e.; due to a negative acknowledgment or no acknowledgment sent by the client). The default is -1 (redeliver until acknowledged). Messages that have reached the maximum delivery count will stay in the stream.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_DELIVER+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__MAX_DELIVER+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|long
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-backoff]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-backoff[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".backoff+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".backoff+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
A sequence of delays controlling the re-delivery of messages on acknowledgment timeout (but not on nak). The sequence length must be less than or equal to MaxDeliver. If backoff is not set; a timeout will result in immediate re-delivery. E.g.; MaxDeliver=5 backoff=++[++5s; 30s; 300s; 3600s; 84000s++]++ will re-deliver a message 5 times over one day. When MaxDeliver is larger than the backoff list; the last delay in the list will apply for the remaining deliveries. Note that backoff is NOT applied to naked messages. A nak will result in immediate re-delivery unless nakWithDelay is used to set the re-delivery delay explicitly. When BackOff is set; it overrides AckWait entirely. The first value in the BackOff determines the AckWait value.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__BACKOFF+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__BACKOFF+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|list of link:https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/time/Duration.html[Duration]
|

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-replay-policy]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-replay-policy[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".replay-policy+++`]##

config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".replay-policy+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
If the policy is ReplayOriginal; the messages in the stream will be pushed to the client at the same rate they were originally received; simulating the original timing. If the policy is ReplayInstant (default); the messages will be pushed to the client as fast as possible while adhering to the acknowledgment policy; Max Ack Pending; and the client's ability to consume those messages.


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__REPLAY_POLICY+++[]

endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
a|`instant`, `original`
|`+++instant+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-push-options-flow-control]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-push-options-flow-control[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".push-options.flow-control+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".push-options.flow-control+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
Enables per-subscription flow control using a sliding-window protocol. This protocol relies on the server and client exchanging messages to regulate when and how many messages are pushed to the client. This one-to-one flow control mechanism works in tandem with the one-to-many flow control imposed by MaxAckPending across all subscriptions bound to a consumer


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_FLOW_CONTROL+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_FLOW_CONTROL+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--
|boolean
|`+++false+++`

a| [[quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-push-options-idle-heartbeat]] [.property-path]##link:#quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging-nats-data-sources-named-datasource-consumers-consumers-push-options-idle-heartbeat[`+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".push-options.idle-heartbeat+++`]##
ifdef::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]
config_property_copy_button:+++quarkus.messaging.nats.data-sources."named-datasource".consumers."consumers".push-options.idle-heartbeat+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-config-props[]


[.description]
--
If the idle heartbeat period is set, the server will regularly send a status message to the client (i.e. when the period has elapsed) while there are no new messages to send. This lets the client know that the JetStream service is still up and running, even when there is no activity on the stream. The message status header will have a code of 100. Unlike FlowControl, it will have no reply to address. It may have a description such ++\++"Idle Heartbeat++\++". Note that this heartbeat mechanism is all handled transparently by supported clients and does not need to be handled by the application


ifdef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: env_var_with_copy_button:+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_IDLE_HEARTBEAT+++[]
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
ifndef::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
Environment variable: `+++QUARKUS_MESSAGING_NATS_DATA_SOURCES__NAMED_DATASOURCE__CONSUMERS__CONSUMERS__PUSH_OPTIONS_IDLE_HEARTBEAT+++`
endif::add-copy-button-to-env-var[]
--

ifndef::no-duration-note[]
[NOTE]
[id=duration-note-anchor-quarkus-nats-jetstream-client_quarkus-messaging]
.About the Duration format
====
To write duration values, use the standard `java.time.Duration` format.
See the link:https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/time/Duration.html#parse(java.lang.CharSequence)[Duration#parse() Java API documentation] for more information.

You can also use a simplified format, starting with a number:

* If the value is only a number, it represents time in seconds.
* If the value is a number followed by `ms`, it represents time in milliseconds.

In other cases, the simplified format is translated to the `java.time.Duration` format for parsing:

* If the value is a number followed by `h`, `m`, or `s`, it is prefixed with `PT`.
* If the value is a number followed by `d`, it is prefixed with `P`.
====
endif::no-duration-note[]
